# W2 cold-chain technical design â€” 2026-10-17

**Decision:** `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`. Architect contract for W2-00 and W2-01..04; Planner sequences and tests these choices. Proposed screen scope still needs owner confirmation. Existing W1 selected-window, source-label, money, Analysis and honest-gap contracts apply by reference (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:1-32`). No mock number is a stored fact.

## 1. W2-00: ordinary source-to-evidence format

The active authenticated path uses **SourceEnvelopeV3**, not the older staged v1/v2 envelope: v3 names record families but refuses them, while S2 also refuses non-measurements (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,77-78,240-246`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:268-280`). Extend that path as schema **4**, `assetops.source-envelope`; retain v3's allocated `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` message identity, canonical JSON, UTC instants and exact numeric text. `source_envelope.py` v1/v2 and existing Stage artifacts remain byte-for-byte readable. The v4 parser accepts v4 only; a version dispatcher accepts v3 and v4. v3 receipts, dedup keys and accepted rows are untouched. A new publication profile pins v4; never switch an existing profile in place (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,70-78`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:87-107`).

| v4 family / kind | Exact payload keys in addition to common v3 top-level fields | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MEASUREMENT` | v3 payload unchanged: `family,measurement,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason`. `STATE_AT_INSTANT` or `INTERVAL_MEAN`; cold point Â°C uses `degC`; state members use `state`; power mean `kW`. | No new measurement class. Null value only with `UNAVAILABLE`; `SUSPECT` excluded from assertions. |
| `EVENT` / `DOOR_OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSE`, `START_REQUEST`, `START_RESULT`, `TRANSFER_RESULT`, `POWER_LOSS`, `POWER_RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOG` | `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,unit,value,quality`. `event_id` and `correlation_id` required identifiers; `component_id` required except Site power events; `actor` = `CONTROLLER`,`SENSOR`,`OPERATOR`; `outcome` = `REQUESTED`,`SUCCEEDED`,`FAILED`,`OPEN`,`CLOSED`,`LOST`,`RESTORED`,`OBSERVED`,`UNKNOWN`, constrained per kind; `unit/value` either both null or a permitted quantitative pair (manual log `degC` only); `quality` = `GOOD` or `SUSPECT`. | An event is a witnessed public observation, not private disturbance truth. `MANUAL_LOG` needs registered operator source and stays contextual unless calibrated to the probe role. |
| `EVENT` / `EV_SESSION_START`, `EV_SESSION_END`, `EV_SESSION_ABORT`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULT`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEAR` | Same keys plus `session_id,connector_id`; both required for session kinds, `connector_id` required for fault/clear and `session_id` nullable there. `outcome` = `STARTED`,`COMPLETED`,`ABORTED`,`FAULTED`,`CLEARED`; `value/unit` null. | Session identity scoped to `(tenant,site,charger asset,connector asset,session_id)`; no vehicle/customer identity. |

Kind-to-outcome allowlist is exact: `DOOR_OPENâ†’OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSEâ†’CLOSED`, `START_REQUESTâ†’REQUESTED`, `START_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `TRANSFER_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `POWER_LOSSâ†’LOST`, `POWER_RESTOREDâ†’RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOGâ†’OBSERVED`, `EV_SESSION_STARTâ†’STARTED`, `EV_SESSION_ENDâ†’COMPLETED`, `EV_SESSION_ABORTâ†’ABORTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULTâ†’FAULTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEARâ†’CLEARED`. All other pairs refuse `INVALID_PAYLOAD`. `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/`degC`; every other event requires both null. A record kind with no `session_id` still carries the key as null; `connector_id` is likewise explicit null for cold events.

Every v4 top-level object keeps the v3 exact keys; `message` keeps `message_id,sequence_epoch,sequence`, `source` and `mapping` keep v3 keys, `timing` keeps `source_time,published_at,interval_start,interval_end`. Events require null interval bounds; their source time is the observed transition, not a duration. For cold event `correlation_id` is the controller outage/attempt ID; a door pair shares its door-cycle ID. For EV, `correlation_id=session_id` on session events and optional registered fault episode ID on fault/clear. `event_id` is stable under retransmission and unique within `(site,source,record_kind)`; message ID remains transport identity. Canonical event payload contains explicit nulls for inapplicable fields. No arbitrary JSON detail, free text, Lab run ID, oracle, private cause, or stock identity. Event IDs generated from frozen scenario + public device channel + transition ordinal in Lab; physical adapters preserve vendor stable event ID or derive a documented hash from raw immutable event key. Cross-source event correlation is a claim input, never an acceptance dedup key.

| Boundary | Exact change / refusal |
| --- | --- |
| Parser and mapper | Add `source_envelope_v4.py` and golden vectors; keep v3 code frozen. Extend mapping entry with `allowed_record_kinds`, `allowed_outcomes`, `requires_correlation`, `requires_session`, `allowed_units`; v3 mapping snapshots parse without these keys. New `simulator-lab-gateway-cold` v1 only allows the channel list in Â§4. Refuse `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`, `MISSING_FIELD`, `UNKNOWN_FIELD`, `WRONG_TYPE`, `INVALID_IDENTITY`, `INVALID_TIMING`, `INVALID_PAYLOAD`, `NOT_CANONICAL`, `MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE`, `UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE`, `RECORD_KIND_UNSUPPORTED`, `CORRELATION_INVALID`. A v4 kind under v3 is `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`. |
| S2 intake | Dispatch by stated schema only; validate auth scope, pinned binding revision, role/asset/kind/unit and source time within Foundation/binding validity before accept. Same message ID + same SHA returns duplicate disposition; same message ID + different SHA is `MESSAGE_ID_CONFLICT`; a second `(site,source,record_kind,event_id)` with different SHA is `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and quarantined; identical event under a new message ID is duplicate event, not a second accepted fact. Contradictory distinct events remain accepted but lower analytic capability. Receipt V2 counts and ordinal dispositions remain stable (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger_service.py:79-167`; `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`). |
| Accepted storage/read | `accepted_evidence.family` becomes `MEASUREMENT|EVENT`; for EVENT, `measurement,conversion_kind` become nullable, while event quality remains `GOOD|SUSPECT`; original/normalized value and unit null unless quantitative manual log. Typed event columns store identity/kind/correlation/actor/outcome, not merely envelope JSON. Keep exact `envelope_text`, SHA, accepted-as Foundation/binding, received/published/source times and S4-05 publication lineage. `/evidence/records` adds `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,session_id,connector_id,outcome` and family/kind filters; old records serialize nulls. No event is converted into a fake state reading (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:54-59`). |

## 2. Schema and migration ledger

Postgres migration head is `0019_verification_core` (`backend/migrations/versions/0019_verification_core.py:1-29`). All new rows carry `(tenant_id,site_id)` and composite same-Site FKs. Use `ledger_append_only()` for facts/revisions, and the existing S1 effective/recorded validity rules for Foundation and binding publications (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:83-125`). No data backfill invents events or observations.

| Migration | Exact schema delta and constraints/indexes | Downgrade |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0020_w2_source_event` (W2-00) | `accepted_evidence`: nullable `record_kind text`, `event_id text`, `correlation_id text`, `session_id text`, `connector_id text`, `actor text`, `outcome text`; relax `measurement`, `conversion_kind`, `original_unit`, `normalized_unit` to nullable; keep `quality` NOT NULL. CHECK `family='MEASUREMENT'` requires old nonnull shape and event columns null, `family='EVENT'` requires kind/event/correlation/actor/outcome/quality and measurement/conversion null, event-specific null/pair rules. CHECK allowed kind/outcome and ID length 1..128; partial UNIQUE `(tenant_id,site_id,source_id,record_kind,event_id)` where family EVENT; index `(tenant_id,site_id,record_kind,correlation_id,source_time,id)` and `(tenant_id,site_id,session_id,source_time,id)` partial EVENT; existing message unique and composite FKs remain. Existing append-only trigger covers columns. `evidence_rejection.kind` accepts event conflict codes; recompute queue domain check includes `cold` (if constrained). | Refuse if any v4 event row, event rejection or cold job exists; then drop indexes/columns, restore NOT NULL and old checks. Never cascade evidence. |
| `0021_w2_cold_config` (W2-01) | Closed Python vocabularies: `SITE_TYPES` keeps `COLDCHAIN`, later adds `EV_CHARGING`; component types add `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`,`COMPRESSOR`,`TRANSFER_SWITCH` (and reserve `EV_CHARGER`,`EV_CONNECTOR` for W2-05a); `RATING_UNITS` adds `degC` while `SIGNAL_UNITS` already has it (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/models.py:26-50,262-264`). DB site-type CHECK already allows cold and EV (`backend/migrations/versions/0001_foundation_identity.py:66`); this migration is a no-op revision anchoring the code-owned vocabulary and fixture check. | No SQL data change; old-role readers remain supported. |
| `0022_w2_cold_assessment` (W2-03) | Reuse `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision` (`0007`, `0009`). Add `cold_episode` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site, `kind` `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`, `asset_id uuid`, `anchor_time timestamptz`, `finding_id uuid NULL`, `created_at timestamptz`, unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)`, scoped asset/Finding FKs); `cold_episode_revision` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site/episode, `revision_no int>0`, `assessment_revision_id uuid`, `basis_sha256 char(64)`, `claim_level text`, `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end timestamptz NULL`, `duration_low/high numeric NULL`, `degree_minutes_low/high numeric NULL`, `peak_observed numeric NULL`, `coverage numeric(5,4)`, `missing_steps jsonb array`, `created_at timestamptz`, CHECK ordered bounds/nonnegative intervals/null for unknown, unique `(episode_id,revision_no)` and `(episode_id,basis_sha256)`, scoped FKs); `cold_episode_evidence` (`episode_revision_id uuid`, tenant/site, `evidence_id uuid`, `use` `SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED`, `reason text NULL`, PK `(episode_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, scoped FKs). Index revisions `(site_id,episode_id,revision_no DESC)` and episodes `(site_id,kind,anchor_time)`; append-only triggers on all three. Existing `recompute_job` supports cold domain, enqueue on accepted cold role/event including late records. | Refuse while cold rows or dependent Findings exist; drop new tables/indexes only. |
| `0023_w2_cold_money` (W2-04) | Reuse `financial_assumption_revision`/assignment and `financial_consequence_revision` lineage (`0010`,`0011`). Add `cold_consequence_revision` (`id uuid PK`, tenant/site, `finding_revision_id uuid`, `episode_revision_id uuid`, `revision_no int>0`, `basis_sha256 char(64)`, `kind` `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`, `quantity_unit` `degC_min|currency_record`, `quantity_low/high numeric NULL`, `amount_low/high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`, `state` `UNPRICED|WITHHELD|RECORDED`, `reason text`, `overlap_group text`, `source_gate text`, `recorded_at timestamptz`; CHECK positive ordered bounds, amount/currency null unless RECORDED, `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` never RECORDED in W2, scoped FKs; unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)`), `cold_consequence_evidence` (`consequence_revision_id`, tenant/site, `evidence_id`, PK pair, scoped FKs). Index `(site_id,recorded_at DESC)` and `(finding_revision_id,revision_no DESC)`; append-only triggers. The existing `finding` table has generator-specific columns (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-61`): W2-03 must add a **nullable** `subject_asset_id uuid` scoped asset FK and a CHECK requiring it for `domain='COLDCHAIN'`; keep `generator_asset_id` as historical text but make it nullable for cold rows, with a CHECK retaining it for `domain='DISPATCH'`. Do this inside `0022`, index `(tenant_id,site_id,domain,subject_asset_id,anchor_start)`, and update Finding read/store code to select by domain. Downgrade refuses if cold Findings exist. The shared `financial_consequence_revision` is fuel-only by CHECK (`backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`), so the cold typed table avoids weakening old fuel semantics. The `0020` event uniqueness must be enforced transactionally against accepted rows; race losers receive a conflict disposition. No application-only uniqueness check.

## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract

Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

| Role / asset type | Kind, unit, cardinality | Need |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `chamber_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required, one calibrated probe per `COLD_ROOM` via declared probe-to-room link |
| `cold_policy_low`,`cold_policy_high` / `COLD_ROOM` | Foundation control number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required; low < high, same effective revision |
| `compressor_state` / `COMPRESSOR` | point state `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, PER_ASSET | required for cooling claim |
| `compressor_power` / `COMPRESSOR` | interval mean `kW`, PER_ASSET | optional corroboration |
| `grid_state` / `METER` | point state `AVAILABLE|LOST`, ONE | required for outage step |
| `backup_request`,`backup_result` / `GENERATOR` | EVENT `START_REQUEST`,`START_RESULT`, ONE per attempt | required for start-response claim |
| `generator_state` / `GENERATOR` | point state `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, ONE | required for observed running state |
| `backup_bus_state` / `AC_BUS` | point state `POWERED|UNPOWERED`, ONE | required for supply-to-cooling claim |
| `transfer_result` / `TRANSFER_SWITCH` | EVENT `TRANSFER_RESULT`, ONE per attempt | optional: absent blocks switch-position claim |
| `door_event` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT open/close, PER_ASSET | optional context |
| `ambient_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context, never substitutes chamber probe |
| `manual_temperature` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT manual log `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context; never silently closes a probe gap |

Foundation topology uses explicit `COLD_ROOM`â†’`TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`â†’room, busâ†’compressor, generatorâ†’transferâ†’bus relations; every referenced asset must exist, one chamber probe cannot be primary for two rooms, and cycles/duplicate edges are refused. Policy band belongs to a room and is effective for the selected window; changing it creates a Foundation revision and binding set selection, never an in-place update. Validate finite exact positive thermal parameters, `0<=hysteresis<high-low`, probe error `>=0`, timeout `>0`, and cadence 5 minutes for the first Lab profile. Publication readiness must say **â€œAdd a chamber temperature probe and bind its Â°C readingâ€**, **â€œSet a lower and upper temperature policy for this roomâ€**, **â€œBind the generator start request and resultâ€**, or **â€œTransfer position is not reported; switch state is unknownâ€** as applicable. Configuration readiness, commissioning evidence and gateway contact remain separate (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_service.py:267-317,674-715`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:32-53`).

## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication

Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. Explicit external electrical port supplies bus power; if composing with mini-grid, use its actual port output and no duplicate generator truth.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

`config/scenarios/cold-normal.yaml`, `cold-excursion.yaml`, `cold-backup-failed.yaml`, `cold-missing-probe.yaml`, `cold-late-reading.yaml`, `cold-contradictory.yaml` all use schema `cold-scenario/1`, 2026-10-06 19:00â€“20:00 UTC, seed 20417, and explicit forcing list ordered `(time,kind,asset)`; reject duplicate conflicting forcing at one instant. `cold-publication/1` channels: chamber probe `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; ambient probe optional every 5; grid, generator, compressor, bus states on transition plus 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per 5-minute closed-open slot; door/start/transfer/power/manual EVENT channels on transition only. All state channels map to explicit finite members and v4 mapping allowlist; no blanket forwarding. Observation profile `cold-observation/1` freezes bias, dropout intervals and latency (3 seconds by default) before Draft. Normal stays within policy and starts backup; excursion has grid loss + failed start then rising chamber temperature; backup chain success and failure variants show every observed step; missing probe drops numeric samples; late reading holds one already generated envelope and releases it later with unchanged source time; contradictory emits a second, independently sourced incompatible state at one time or an invalid role/kind mapping. Never force target mock numbers: replay assertions use observed bounds.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings

Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. If two good primary readings disagree by >0.4Â°C at same timestamp, `CONTRADICTORY` and no point decision. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

Recommend the IDs/tier above; allow actual deterministic accepted output to change displayed numbers rather than forcing the simulator to fit the mock.

## Domain seams and claim rules

Reuse tenant/Site identity, immutable time-valid Foundation and binding revisions, source registrations, typed `EvidenceQuery`, S2 AcceptedEvidence/receipt, selected half-open window and knowledge cutoff, revisioned Finding/financial consequence, S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance, W1 Site shell and separate Analysis destination (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-84`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:22-54,173-183`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-19,4165-4188`). Keep `COLDCHAIN` and `EV_CHARGING` as explicit site types; bind each physical source signal to asset, semantic role, unit, cadence, valid period and mapping/Foundation revision. A signal cannot fill two incompatible roles by label alone. Historical revisions remain inspectable; absent/stale/contradictory roles produce claim-specific Unknown. No status is inferred from gateway contact alone.

| Contract | Cold-chain | EV charging |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Foundation/roles | Chamber/probe and policy low/high Â°C required for excursion; compressor run/power, grid import/status, generator request/state, backup bus or inverter state required **per causal step**. Door, defrost, ambient, load and transfer position optional context whose absence blocks those explanations. Policy and calibration versioned; readings in UTC with Site-local display. | Connector identity and enabled schedule, session ID/start/end/outcome, interval meter kWh, connector status, site import kW and effective site/charger limits. Request/current limit and vehicle demand optional for power-constraint diagnosis; tariff/billing independent. Require declared status cadence and connector schedule for availability, which W2 does not promise. |
| Evidence/assessment | Validated Â°C samples and policy yield crossing interval `[last in-band, first out-of-band]` and return interval; duration/Â°CÂ·min lower/upper bounds, coverage, peak **observed** value. Missing probe segment cannot be bridged. Backup chain matches ordered accepted events within declared timestamp tolerance, reports each missing step and never infers starter-part fault from timing. Distinguish temperature excursion, observed failed automatic start, and unknown cause. | Correlate records by scoped charger/connector/session ID; duplicate/conflicting IDs quarantine. Start + end + accepted metered intervals establish delivered energy; observed fault/stop establishes abort; absent end remains incomplete. Summaries keep complete/aborted/incomplete denominators and known/unknown time. Below-limit site import is context, not proof that the site supply did not constrain a session without synchronized request/limit/voltage evidence. |
| Finding/currentness | Separate excursion and start-response Finding identities/revisions, exact evidence IDs, configuration and model versions; both may cite the same event but no duplicated money. Late evidence queues recomputation and visibly marks previous revision stale until rebuilt. | Session-interruption Finding cites exact fault/stop/meter evidence; incomplete sessions are evidence gaps, not failures. Late end/status can supersede through a new revision; missing data never silently becomes normal. |
| Money | Excursion â†’ unpriced **potential product exposure** unless stock identity, quantity/value and a reviewed product-specific exposure-response rule are present; temperature alone never proves spoilage. Recorded call-out/repair expense needs an actual work/cost record. No summed loss across overlapping backup and excursion Findings. | Metered supported energy Ã— versioned tariff gives gross *scenario* estimate, not collections, margin or lost revenue. Lost revenue requires credible counterfactual demand, price and service baseline; W2 keeps it unpriced. Warranty/repair scenarios wait for asset/work contracts. |
| Readiness/SLD | Reuse S1 configuration readiness and S7 source health separately. Draw declared grid/PV/battery/control bus/generator/compressor/probe links; label inferred electrical-to-cooling relationship as configuration, not live flow. | Draw effective site feed/meter/charger/connector links and configured limits; no stationary battery unless Foundation declares one. SLD monitoring markers derive from bindings + source health, never drawing alone. |

Both analytics consume only normalized AcceptedEvidence with quality, source/measurement/receive times, role and effective configuration. Output stores input digest (accepted IDs/content, mapping/Foundation, window and model version), bounded quantities, reasons and projection watermark. Clock skew, low cadence, gaps and contradiction can independently lower capability. Current-money reads retain LR and S4-05 source gates; `Generated test data` and `Prepared example data` labels are adjacent to material claim limits, mixed/unknown suppress current amounts (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:4165-4188`). W1 plain default with chart/timeline and contextual detail applies from each slice; no raw codes/IDs or simulator terms in the default reading (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-15`).

## Simulator-to-evidence contract

1. Add **versioned cold pack** to `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/`: thermal stock (chamber/ambient heat exchange and load), door-opening heat pulse, compressor on/off and cooling capacity, control power versus compressor power, grid-loss/backup transfer and generator start/failure. Compose with existing electrical mini-grid machinery only at explicit power ports; no event timeline written directly into reports. Declare discrete states/transitions and initialization; step alignment and conservation/temperature sanity guards are executable. Private world has disturbance cause, target trajectory and oracle; public device transform sees only probe, meter, controller and manual-log observations (`simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/model.py:1-110`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/mini_grid.py:1-100`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/publish.py:1-45`).
2. Add **versioned EV pack**: charger/connector availability, requests, negotiated limit, site shared-power cap, session start/energy/end/fault state, meter energy integration. A vehicle-request schedule is private forcing, not accepted demand evidence. Device transform publishes charger events/status, interval meter and site meter separately. Simulated missing end/status is a publication-path loss, not a fabricated `FAILED` event.
3. Extend `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` and registered Lab basis with exact pack/model version, Foundation values, role/source mapping, report cadence, observation bias/gaps, gateway publication schedule and frozen scenario digest; reject unsupported states before Draft. Add `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` and `ev-*.yaml` plus profile versions. Stage seals public envelopes; registered Commit binds run/batch before ordinary gateway release. Continue through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2, S2 receipt and AcceptedEvidence; no direct Finding or evidence seed and no private manifest in product bytes (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-75`; `host/gateway_publication.py:1-25`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).
4. One deterministic replay manifest per case freezes Site/Foundation/binding IDs, scenario/profile/model/observation versions, UTC window, random seed if used, expected public batch/envelope digests, receipt dispositions, accepted IDs and bounded assessment assertions. Cases per domain: **positive** excursion/start failure and observed EV abort; **negative** in-policy/normal start and completed sessions; **missing** probe/transfer and end/status; **late** delayed accepted reports forcing new revision, with prior read stale. Include contradictory clock/role mapping refusal. Re-run same bytes for idempotent receipts and compare model-independent public physical-adapter peer after typed normalization. A labelled vendor-format fixture is a contract peer, not attested live source; actual physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-demo-data-plan-proposal.md:3-17`; `frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79-86`).

## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication

Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. Explicit external electrical port supplies bus power; if composing with mini-grid, use its actual port output and no duplicate generator truth.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

`config/scenarios/cold-normal.yaml`, `cold-excursion.yaml`, `cold-backup-failed.yaml`, `cold-missing-probe.yaml`, `cold-late-reading.yaml`, `cold-contradictory.yaml` all use schema `cold-scenario/1`, 2026-10-06 19:00â€“20:00 UTC, seed 20417, and explicit forcing list ordered `(time,kind,asset)`; reject duplicate conflicting forcing at one instant. `cold-publication/1` channels: chamber probe `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; ambient probe optional every 5; grid, generator, compressor, bus states on transition plus 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per 5-minute closed-open slot; door/start/transfer/power/manual EVENT channels on transition only. All state channels map to explicit finite members and v4 mapping allowlist; no blanket forwarding. Observation profile `cold-observation/1` freezes bias, dropout intervals and latency (3 seconds by default) before Draft. Normal stays within policy and starts backup; excursion has grid loss + failed start then rising chamber temperature; backup chain success and failure variants show every observed step; missing probe drops numeric samples; late reading holds one already generated envelope and releases it later with unchanged source time; contradictory emits a second, independently sourced incompatible state at one time or an invalid role/kind mapping. Never force target mock numbers: replay assertions use observed bounds.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings

Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. If two good primary readings disagree by >0.4Â°C at same timestamp, `CONTRADICTORY` and no point decision. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

Do not promote registry nodes until independent slice review and the owner's scope decision. The existing registry stays unchanged.
"""Domain records for Site configuration templates and for Sites.

Two identity spaces live here and they never merge. A template has
`template_id` and `template_version`, no `site_id`, no lifecycle status, no
location, and no place-bound timezone; it cannot be listed as a Site, targeted
by a scenario, simulated, or receive evidence. A Site has `site_id` and nothing
else is its identity - not its display name, not the template it came from, and
not the shell it was created in.

Neither record type is a base, mode, or parameterization of the other, and the
Site records below repeat rather than reuse the template component shape,
because a Site's Foundation is a copy taken at creation time.

The Foundation content on both is component truth declared by a document. It is
not evidence: that a template or a Site declares a 100 kW PV array is not a
claim that any array exists, is commissioned, or has ever reported a
measurement.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

# The M1 Site types. A template declares which kind of Site it would produce;
# that is a statement about the archetype, not about any Site.
SITE_TYPES = frozenset({"MINIGRID", "COLDCHAIN"})

# Component archetypes a template may declare. Strict and closed: an
# unsupported component type is a configuration error, not a passthrough
# string, because later slices bind topology, devices, and signal mappings to
# these values.
COMPONENT_TYPES = frozenset(
    {
        "PV_ARRAY",
        "INVERTER",
        "BATTERY",
        "POWER_CONVERSION_SYSTEM",
        "GENERATOR",
        "FUEL_TANK",
        "AC_BUS",
        "LOAD",
        "COLD_ROOM",
        "METER",
    }
)

# Units a declared design rating may use. Also closed, for the same reason:
# a canonical unit vocabulary is what later telemetry mapping is checked
# against.
RATING_UNITS = frozenset({"kW", "kWh", "kVA", "V", "A", "Hz", "L"})

# --- Typed component properties ---------------------------------------------
#
# T020A adds the second thing a Foundation may say about a component. A
# `Rating` is the one nameplate magnitude a component was sold with; a
# property is one named, typed, unit-carrying physical or control fact about
# that same component, and a component may declare several.
#
# The vocabulary below is CLOSED, in the same way `RATING_UNITS` and
# `SIGNAL_UNITS` are closed and for the same reason: a model profile binds to
# a property by name, so a document that could coin its own property keys
# could coin a key no profile will ever look for, and a run would block on a
# value that looks declared. A slice that needs a new property adds it here.
#
# The unit is the vocabulary's, not the author's. Specific fuel consumption is
# `L/kWh` because that is the machine's own property
# (`D-2026-09-22-consumption-coefficient-unit`); a document declaring it in
# `L/h` is declaring a number that is only true at one operating point, and
# the parser refuses it rather than storing it and letting a later consumer
# find out.

#: What kind of fact a property is. `PHYSICAL` is something the machine is;
#: `CONTROL` is a configured control value about it.
#:
#: `CONTROL` here is an inspectable typed property and nothing more. It is not
#: a controller, not a setpoint a run may write, and not a Site-scoped
#: Controls capability: the first controller that CONSUMES one of these
#: arrives with T024, and the T016 restriction on control-state vocabulary is
#: untouched - no switching position, no breaker state, no operating mode.
COMPONENT_PROPERTY_KINDS = frozenset({"PHYSICAL", "CONTROL"})

#: Where a declared property came from. Same two values, and the same meaning,
#: as `CONTROL_ASSUMPTION_BASES`: `TEMPLATE` is a property the archetype
#: declared and the Site copied, `SITE` is one declared for this Site.
COMPONENT_PROPERTY_SOURCES = frozenset({"TEMPLATE", "SITE"})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PropertyDefinition:
    """One member of the closed property vocabulary.

    The unit belongs to the definition rather than to the document: a property
    key names a quantity, and a quantity has one canonical unit here for the
    same reason a scenario parameter does.
    """

    unit: str
    kind: str
    display_name: str


#: The closed property vocabulary. Four members, which is what this slice's
#: minimum physical proof and its component-control demonstration need.
#:
#: `tank-capacity` and `specific-fuel-consumption` are the physical pair the
#: first causal kernel reads out of a frozen run. `reserve-state-of-charge`
#: and `minimum-runtime` are the control pair: typed, inspectable, and
#: consumed by nothing in this build.
COMPONENT_PROPERTY_DEFINITIONS: dict[str, PropertyDefinition] = {
    "tank-capacity": PropertyDefinition(
        unit="L", kind="PHYSICAL", display_name="Tank capacity"
    ),
    "specific-fuel-consumption": PropertyDefinition(
        unit="L/kWh",
        kind="PHYSICAL",
"""S4-02a phase 1: Site financial assumption revisions and bitemporal assignments.

`D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis`; packet `tasks/S4-02a-site-assumptions.md`
"Persistence, publication and read contract" items 1-2.

`financial_assumption_revision` is one immutable, explicitly authored Site price
(value, currency, unit, basis, source, authored `[valid_from, valid_to)` intent,
server `recorded_at`, server actor, channel, authorization basis, provenance,
publication `change_kind` and idempotency key). It is append-only: a price row is
never rewritten.

Effective validity lives in `financial_assumption_assignment` (review carry M1):
each row assigns one revision to `[valid_from, valid_to)` over recorded time
`[recorded_from, recorded_to)`. Rows are immutable except a set-once
`recorded_to` (as `foundation_revision.superseded_at`). The GiST exclusion over
both axes means no two assignments of a Site/key ever overlap in valid time at
any recorded instant, so a superseded row stays selectable at older cutoffs. A
two-table trigger requires every assignment's valid range to lie inside its
revision's authored range, and its recorded start to be no earlier than the
revision's `recorded_at` nor than the series' latest `recorded_from`.

Revision ID: 0010_financial_assumptions
Revises: 0009_finding_store
Create Date: 2026-10-13
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from alembic import op

revision = "0010_financial_assumptions"
down_revision = "0009_finding_store"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None


SCHEMA = r"""
CREATE TABLE financial_assumption_revision (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    assumption_key text NOT NULL CHECK (assumption_key IN ('diesel_price_per_litre')),
    revision_no integer NOT NULL CHECK (revision_no >= 1),
    prior_revision_id uuid NULL,
    currency text NOT NULL CHECK (currency ~ '^[A-Z]{3}$'),
    unit text NOT NULL CHECK (unit = 'currency/L'),
    -- Explicitly authored, nonnegative and finite: numeric admits 'NaN' and
    -- 'Infinity', and NaN sorts above every number, so both bounds are needed.
    value numeric NOT NULL CHECK (value >= 0 AND value < 'Infinity'::numeric),
    basis text NOT NULL CHECK (basis IN ('AUTHORED_ESTIMATE','CONTRACT_RATE','RECORDED_PURCHASE')),
    source_reference text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(source_reference)) > 0),
    valid_from timestamptz NOT NULL,
    valid_to timestamptz NULL,
    recorded_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    change_kind text NOT NULL CHECK (change_kind IN ('INITIAL','SUCCESSION','CORRECTION')),
    correction_reason text NULL,
    actor_kind text NOT NULL CHECK (actor_kind IN ('SYSTEM','HUMAN')),
    actor_id text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(actor_id)) > 0),
    author_id uuid NULL,
    channel text NOT NULL CHECK (channel IN ('SYSTEM_CLI','SYSTEM_FIXTURE','HUMAN_API')),
    authorization_basis text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(authorization_basis)) > 0),
    provenance jsonb NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(idempotency_key)) > 0),
    request_sha256 text NOT NULL CHECK (request_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_site_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id) REFERENCES site(tenant_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_no_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, assumption_key, revision_no),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_publication_key_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, assumption_key, idempotency_key),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_scope_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, assumption_key, id),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_prior_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, assumption_key, prior_revision_id)
        REFERENCES financial_assumption_revision(tenant_id, site_id, assumption_key, id),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_first CHECK ((revision_no = 1) = (prior_revision_id IS NULL)),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_valid_range CHECK (valid_to IS NULL OR valid_from < valid_to),
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_correction_reason CHECK (
        (change_kind = 'CORRECTION') = (correction_reason IS NOT NULL AND length(btrim(correction_reason)) > 0)),
    -- No client-supplied author: a human author exists only with a verified human principal.
    CONSTRAINT financial_assumption_revision_actor CHECK (
        (actor_kind = 'HUMAN') = (author_id IS NOT NULL)
        AND (actor_kind = 'HUMAN') = (channel = 'HUMAN_API'))
);
CREATE INDEX financial_assumption_revision_recorded
    ON financial_assumption_revision (tenant_id, site_id, assumption_key, recorded_at);

CREATE TABLE financial_assumption_assignment (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
"""S4-02b phase 1: append-only financial consequence revisions, lineage links and durable dirty work.

`D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis` (and its `fuel-cost-1` addendum); packet
`tasks/S4-02b-fuel-consequence.md` "Migration, recompute and reads". This supersedes the technical design's
single-assessment / single-assumption / scalar-quantity sketch (review carry L1): rows carry explicit
`quantity_low/high`, a separate technical-input digest and consequence basis digest, and multi-row links.

- `financial_consequence_revision`: one immutable `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST` consequence of one exact
  FindingRevision, `MODELLED_CONDITIONAL`, with the `fuel-cost-1` parameter version, quantity bounds, the
  whole-window price selection and nullable amount bounds. CHECKs make an unavailable quantity carry no
  number, an unpriced consequence carry no amount and a priced one carry exactly `0 .. quantity_high x price`.
  UNIQUE `(finding_revision_id, consequence_type, revision_no)` and `(finding_revision_id, consequence_type,
  consequence_basis_sha256)` (idempotent append).
- Links: `financial_consequence_assessment_revision` (primary plus every supporting exact assessment, each
  required to be linked by the FindingRevision itself), `financial_consequence_assumption_revision` (every
  exact price revision used) and `financial_consequence_power_interval` (each counted accepted power mean once,
  with power, duration, SFC revision, covered Candidate spans and containment result).
- Dirty/outbox: `financial_consequence_work` rows are inserted only by triggers, in the same transaction as an
  assumption publication or a FindingRevision append, keyed by Site, key/revision or FindingRevision and the
  affected valid range. `financial_consequence_work_attempt` records each recompute attempt (phase 2a worker);
  work without a SUCCEEDED attempt is pending. FindingRevisions that predate this migration are enqueued once
  by the upgrade itself (review L4), so no Finding is left with neither a consequence nor pending work.

Revision ID: 0011_financial_consequences
Revises: 0010_financial_assumptions
Create Date: 2026-10-14
"""

ALTER TABLE financial_assumption_revision

CREATE TABLE financial_consequence_revision (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    finding_id uuid NOT NULL,
    finding_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    consequence_type text NOT NULL CHECK (consequence_type IN ('POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST')),
    revision_no integer NOT NULL CHECK (revision_no >= 1),
    prior_consequence_revision_id uuid NULL REFERENCES financial_consequence_revision(id),
    window_start timestamptz NOT NULL,
    window_end timestamptz NOT NULL,
    overlap_group text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(overlap_group)) > 0),
    basis_kind text NOT NULL CHECK (basis_kind IN ('MODELLED_CONDITIONAL')),
    model_id text NOT NULL CHECK (model_id IN ('fuel-cost-1')),
    formula_version text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(formula_version)) > 0),
    parameter_version text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(parameter_version)) > 0),
    parameter_sha256 text NOT NULL CHECK (parameter_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    sfc_upper_multiplier numeric NOT NULL CHECK (sfc_upper_multiplier > 0 AND sfc_upper_multiplier < 'Infinity'::numeric),
    state text NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('PRICED','UNPRICED','QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE')),
    quantity_state text NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_state IN ('AVAILABLE','QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE')),
    quantity_reason text NULL,
    quantity_detail text NULL,
    quantity_unit text NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_unit = 'L'),
    quantity_low numeric NULL,
    quantity_high numeric NULL,
    quantity_high_exact text NULL CHECK (quantity_high_exact IS NULL OR quantity_high_exact ~ '^[0-9]+/[1-9][0-9]*$'),
    price_state text NOT NULL CHECK (price_state IN ('PRICED','UNPRICED','NOT_EVALUATED')),
    price_reason text NULL,
    price_revision_id uuid NULL,
    price_value numeric NULL,
    price_currency text NULL CHECK (price_currency IS NULL OR price_currency ~ '^[A-Z]{3}$'),
    -- Review M1: the basis content of the window price selection, sorted window-clipped
    -- [revision_id, from, to] of every assignment overlapping the window (never raw assignment IDs).
    price_considered jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb CHECK (jsonb_typeof(price_considered) = 'array'),
    amount_low numeric NULL,
    amount_high numeric NULL,
    currency text NULL CHECK (currency IS NULL OR currency ~ '^[A-Z]{3}$'),
    limitations jsonb NOT NULL CHECK (jsonb_typeof(limitations) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(limitations) > 0),
    technical_input_sha256 text NOT NULL CHECK (technical_input_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    consequence_basis_sha256 text NOT NULL CHECK (consequence_basis_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    actor_id text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(actor_id)) > 0),
    recorded_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT financial_consequence_window_ordered CHECK (window_start <= window_end),
    CONSTRAINT financial_consequence_prior_iff_not_first CHECK ((revision_no = 1) = (prior_consequence_revision_id IS NULL)),
    -- An unavailable quantity carries no number, no price and no amount.
    CONSTRAINT financial_consequence_unavailable_has_no_number CHECK (
        (state = 'QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE') = (quantity_state = 'QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE')
        AND (quantity_state = 'AVAILABLE' OR (quantity_reason IS NOT NULL AND quantity_low IS NULL
             AND quantity_high IS NULL AND quantity_high_exact IS NULL AND price_state = 'NOT_EVALUATED'))),
    CONSTRAINT financial_consequence_available_bounds CHECK (
        quantity_state = 'QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE' OR (quantity_reason IS NULL AND quantity_low = 0

    -- Unpriced is never zero: no amount, no currency.

        state = 'PRICED' OR (amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL)),
    CONSTRAINT financial_consequence_unpriced_reason CHECK (
        state <> 'UNPRICED' OR (price_state = 'UNPRICED' AND price_reason IS NOT NULL AND price_revision_id IS NULL
            AND price_value IS NULL)),
Addendum to `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis` (S4-02b `fuel-cost-1`): The first reviewed fuel model may use immutable, dimensionless `sfc_upper_multiplier = 1.10` as an expressly conditional assumption that actual L/kWh does not exceed 1.10 times the selected Foundation SFC. Its version records the value, inequality, scope, source, reviewer/date, formula and immutable lineage; it is not a measured or calibrated physical bound. For each exact FindingRevision, quantify only observed Candidate spans fully covered by accepted, good, nonoverlapping generator-power mean intervals, each wholly contained within contiguous Candidate time and carrying one unambiguous positive SFC throughout. Count each distinct interval once: `quantity_low = 0 L`, `quantity_high = 1.10 Ã— Î£(mean kW Ã— full interval hours Ã— SFC L/kWh)`. Never use dispatch interval `upper_minutes`, a segment `max mean Ã— duration`, or prorating of an interval that crosses non-Candidate time. Any failed coverage, containment, power or SFC condition makes the whole consequence `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE` with no amount. The upper is a model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, not measured fuel saved or a physical maximum. Independent model review and the planned owner UI checkpoint still apply. Decision: `D-2026-10-14-action-lifecycle-and-policy-application`. Under the owner's 08:40-10:40 +0300 delegation, the Codex Architect chooses and resolves `A-action-lifecycle` in the 0.54% next-work contest (`.agent/next-work-2026-10-14d.json`: evidence-acceptance consequence work 2.020, Action lifecycle 2.009). S6-01 creates one immutable, tenant/Site-scoped Action identity from a reviewed persisted `FindingRevision` under the canonical Finding route. It pins the originating revision, selected assessment and accepted-evidence/configuration basis; a later Finding revision does not silently rewrite that origin. A Finding may have several Actions, but each accepted recommendation creates or idempotently returns one Action for its server-defined command key. The recommendation is a bounded proposed reserve-SOC policy trial with explicit purpose, limitation and source Finding; it is not a measured saving or verified outcome. The Action detail root is `/sites/{site}/actions/{action_id}` with stable child routes and exact `event_id` selection, and Site/Finding back-links. S6-01 may navigate from a known Finding or Action ID before the separate S6-03 Site ActionIndex exists. Hidden unsupported tabs must not masquerade as empty delivered views.

Action state is derived from an append-only, server-ordered `ActionEvent` ledger. Record stable event ID, Action and Site, event type, `occurred_at`, server `recorded_at`, idempotency key, actor kind/ID, source channel, authorization basis and triggering command/job or content revision. Idempotent identical retries return the event; conflicting reuse and cross-Site references fail. Historical reads select a recorded cutoff and then `(recorded_at,id)` order; a backdated `occurred_at` is displayed as such but never rewrites the audit order. `PROPOSED`/`ACCEPTED`, application `PENDING`/`APPLIED`/`FAILED`, and later verification status are separate dimensions. Acceptance does not assert application; an applied change or completed work does not assert benefit. A failed application preserves its attempt, error and retry relation without an effective-policy claim. No human author, approval or attestation is inferred from a gateway credential, client field, local OS user or SYSTEM fixture. Under the existing `A-actor-identity` rule, human writes need a verified operator principal; otherwise only a clearly labelled, server-authorized SYSTEM demonstration command with stable service/job provenance is allowed, or the write stays unavailable.

The supported S6-01 change is a controlled reserve-SOC edit through S1 Foundation publish, producing a new immutable effective `FoundationRevision` and an immutable change artifact that links Action, accepted recommendation, authorized command, old/new revision IDs, exact field/value/unit, validity and recorded time, and the application event. Validate the expected old revision and Site scope under the publish lock; reject stale, unauthorized or invalid edits and record a failed attempt without claiming the new setting took effect. `APPLIED` requires confirmation of the persisted new effective revision; an ActionEvent alone is insufficient. The old basis and every event/change revision remain addressable after reload. Do not mutate a Lab-only override or rewrite accepted evidence. S6-01's Execution/History and applied `changes:1#a` show policy activity with event and configuration provenance, including pending/failed cases; they do not show an effect verdict.

S6-02 owns the separate immutable `VerificationPlanRevision` and later `VerificationOutcome`. A plan pins this Action, the applied-change event and new Foundation revision, a pre-change baseline assessment/evidence basis, target, service/reserve guardrails, required coverage and later-window selection rule before evaluation. Use separate baseline and later Lab runs under their respective fixed effective Foundation revisions; commit their public reports through the ordinary gateway and central acceptance. Evaluate a closed later accepted window against the frozen plan and baseline, with exact analytic/evidence IDs. Missing or conflicting evidence yields `INCONCLUSIVE`; failed guardrail prevents `VERIFIED` even if the target improves; later results append/supersede without rewriting prior outcomes. Plan/evidence/verification child routes preserve exact plan and outcome IDs. `A-S6-verification` remains open for the full comparison rule; this lifecycle decision does not resolve it. S6-03 alone owns the Site-scoped ActionIndex/list and its watermarked empty/positive states; `A-action-index` remains open. Planner must refine S6-01's event transition table, command/API schema, actor path, race/idempotency checks, Foundation publish seam, stored fixtures and desktop/narrow Action-root states against the v6.9 Action screens and technical design before implementation.

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort`. Accept LR-01 Q1-Q4 as corrected by Follow-up M1 in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md`. Source-time order requires a versioned, scoped clock attestation from an identified authority covering each reading with maximum UTC error no greater than 60 seconds; simulated proof stays separate from real-source proof. Order an attested channel by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) +/- the attested error, not by the full measurement interval widened by error. Measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap; genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remains `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous. Where placement-uncertainty intervals are disjoint, gateway admission sequence disagreement is a late arrival, not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`; sequence still resolves identity and duplicates. Retain epoch ambiguity unless the existing explicit cohort selection is used; automatic cross-epoch continuity needs separate review. Attestation changes enqueue all affected UTC-day windows through the matching six-hour query bracket and neighboring continuity windows, preserving immutable runs and selections. Proven Candidate spans may remain `SUPPORTED`, while a material refused tail produces a new FindingRevision with `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` and `SERIES_LIMIT`. A shared read-derived money eligibility gate withholds current amount during pending reassessment or material unresolved generator-series ambiguity, even if a historical consequence is `CURRENT PRICED`; eligibility alone does not replace the exact-head consequence and source/currentness checks. The Planner must add the corrected overlap rule, requeue scope, eligibility precedence and explicit-epoch boundary to the LR-01 packet before implementation. Authority: `tasks/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit.md`, `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1, and `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`.

A server-authorized, immutable pre-admission publication binds an exact Batch V2 commitment to tenant/Site, gateway/source, external batch ID and canonical digest. `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` additionally binds fixture/spec ID and version, including authored P01 and P02. `REGISTERED_SIMULATION` binds an ordinary simulator batch to a server-recorded run identity; `SIMULATED` mode or a private manifest alone is insufficient. Publication and admission serialize on the scoped batch key; post-admission registration and mismatched commitments fail. Accepted IDs inherit immutable provenance, duplicates retain their original accepted ID, and historical unbound IDs stay unknown. Finding and consequence source state derives from each revision's exact accepted IDs, including consequence power intervals. Coherent all-authored or all-registered-simulation sets may show a current amount only when LR eligibility, exact basis, currentness, source-open and quantity/price gates also pass. Mixed or unknown source blocks a current amount in Finding Financials, consequence and S5 Overview regardless of `CURRENT PRICED` or LR eligibility; prior values remain audit-only. Real data remains unknown and unable to show current money until a separate server-authorized `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` follow-on is implemented. Create open registry node `I-attested-real-source-provenance` in `real_pilot_requires` before resolving `I-stored-demo-provenance` or installing a pilot timer; S4-05 may resolve before the real-source node, but its resolution is not pilot readiness. Amend S5-01's source gate to consume stored exact-revision provenance whichever slice merges second. Extend the existing PostgreSQL evidence-ledger writer, not the writer count: rule 8 permits only its INSERTs to publication/link rows, refuses other writers and mutations, preserves existing writer protections, and is backed by database immutability. Visible copy and pilot designation still require the owner's W1 checkpoint; this decision grants neither owner approval nor W1/pilot readiness.

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Under the owner-absence work order item 9, resolve `A-finding-index` for a tenant/Site/domain and selected half-open UTC window of at most 31 days. Complete discovery requires every intersected UTC-day analytic window and neighbours reachable through 20-minute episode continuity matched through each current selection seq. Only complete current scoped discovery under an open S3 Performance cutover gate may state zero Findings; pending, stale, failed and unassessed reads remain visibly Unknown or historical. A closed gate refuses the unpinned index with HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` and its reason, with neither current rows nor a no-Findings claim, as on Finding routes; S4-05 stored provenance joins that gate once merged. A changed basis invalidates the bound page cursor. Decision: `D-2026-10-16-s6-verification`. Resolve `A-S6-verification` under `D-2026-10-14-action-lifecycle-and-policy-application`, `D-2026-10-15-s601-action-store-and-ui` and the S6-01 reopen hold recorded in `.agent/owner-autonomy-2026-10-15.md` item 9 and merged Action code. `D-2026-10-15-s601-reopen` governs the prior-Action hold. S6-02 freezes a versioned plan before later evidence, comparing separate closed 60-minute baseline and post-effective windows under old and new persisted Foundation revisions, with the same Site, asset, dispatch model, signal roles and predeclared comparability rule. Target is at least 25% less Candidate segment seconds, clipped to each half-open plan window from selected UTC-day `dispatch-3` revisions under S3 bracket/continuity rules; pin exact day revision IDs in the plan selection binding and outcome. Indeterminate or open-bound time is INCONCLUSIVE. Never add an S3 60-minute selection kind or use runtime, money or Finding count as the target. S6-02 adds public requested/delivered critical-load roles and five-minute Lab publication/readiness support; required coverage includes those roles, dispatch roles, cohort/clock basis, SOC and time-valid reserve. No required gap or material conflict may be hidden. Service unserved energy may not increase; later reserve-breach minutes must be zero and not increase; terminal SOC at a comparable horizon may not fall. A missing guardrail is unknown. On a complete pair, target and every guardrail pass yields `VERIFIED`; target or measured guardrail failure yields `INEFFECTIVE`, with `GUARDRAIL_FAIL` as a reason, not an outcome. Missing/ambiguous coverage, comparability, source provenance or unsettled reassessment yields `INCONCLUSIVE` or pending read state; a known failure remains visible without promoting a verdict while other required facts are unknown. Resolve source per window; a compatible pair has the same coherent source class and registered scenario/profile lineage, distinct run digests and intact links. Only `REGISTERED_SIMULATION` or `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` public evidence may support a labelled demo verdict; mixed/unknown source fails closed, and no real-source positive verdict is claimed before real attestation. Late evidence or changed basis withdraws current finality while S3/S4 reassess; append a new immutable outcome when settled, retaining exact plan, assessment, Finding, accepted-envelope and source IDs and old outcomes. The evaluator job appends `RESOLVED` for a final current `VERIFIED` or `INEFFECTIVE`; only VERIFIED or target-miss-only INEFFECTIVE releases the prior-Action hold. Measured reserve/service `GUARDRAIL_FAIL` keeps the Site/asset/field trial blocked with `PRIOR_TRIAL_GUARDRAIL_FAILED` until a future rollback/review contract. `INCONCLUSIVE`, pending and failed application retain the hold. If reassessment invalidates closure, the evaluator job appends `RESOLUTION_WITHDRAWN` and restores the guard atomically with acceptance; a closure/late-reading overlap permanently blocks further acceptance with a typed reason until a future reviewer contract. A changed covering FoundationRevision for either window makes the result pending, then INCONCLUSIVE without a new comparable plan. S6-02 migrates the `action_event` CHECKs for both events and updates `lifecycle.reduce`/`service.is_unresolved` to read latest event under the trial lock; never rewrite history. The system computes metrics and verdict; a server-authorized, labelled SYSTEM demonstration command may request evaluation or plan freeze, never dictate the result or append resolution events. Two fixed-policy Lab runs and success, guardrail-fail and inconclusive variants travel through normal gateway/ingestion; no causal saving is asserted. Non-UI store/rule/read/fixture proof precedes Action Plan/Evidence/Verification and related Recommendation/Changes UI; S6-02 owner visual acceptance remains the owner's. Full implementable contract: `.agent/architect-A-S6-verification-answers.md`.

Decision: `D-2026-10-16-s701-clock`. For the S7-01 v1 two-clock read, a measured absolute gateway clock offset of at most 5000 ms is WITHIN_TOLERANCE; above it is UNTRUSTED.
