+# W2 EV charging technical design — 2026-10-17
+
+**Decision:** `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-ev`. Implementable contract for W2-05a, 05b, 06, 07. Planner sequences and tests it. The W2-00 v4 event format, deduplication, accepted-event columns, 0020 migration and closed host pack registry are fixed by `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:5-36,59-69`; this document adds no new envelope family. Existing W1 selected-window, exact-source, money and plain-language rules apply (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15,4165-4188`). The screen scope and EV visual hierarchy remain owner proposals (`.agent/architect-W2-design.md:1-32,69-81`); no registry promotion follows from this design.
+
+## 1. Migration ledger after 0023
+
+All new IDs are UUID, times `timestamptz` UTC, quantities finite Postgres `numeric` (no floats), SHA fields lowercase 64-character hex. All new rows have `(tenant_id,site_id)` and composite same-Site FKs to parent/asset/evidence/revision; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` wherever a composite FK targets a new table. Use append-only triggers on fact and revision tables, S1 effective/recorded validity for Foundation/bindings, and transactionally enforced uniqueness; never fabricate historical observations (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:38-125`; `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-139`).
+
+| Migration | Exact delta and integrity | Downgrade |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `0024_w2_ev_config` / W2-05a | No new SQL table. Add `EV_CHARGING` to closed `SITE_TYPES`; `EV_CHARGER`,`EV_CONNECTOR` to `COMPONENT_TYPES`; `state` and `s` to typed property units. DB Site-type CHECK from 0001 already permits EV. Register typed Foundation property keys `ev-site-import-limit` kW on Site feed (`AC_BUS`), `ev-charger-max-power` kW, `ev-connector-max-power` kW, `ev-connector-enabled` state, `ev-meter-resolution` kWh, `ev-meter-error-bound` kWh, `ev-status-cadence` min, `ev-meter-cadence` min, `ev-clock-error-bound` s; require explicit values, no implicit defaults for real Sites. Replace 0020's accepted-evidence family CHECK so EV MEASUREMENT may carry paired nonnull `session_id,connector_id` only for `ev_connector_energy`/`INTERVAL_DELTA`, while other measurement rows retain null event columns; `record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,actor,outcome` remain null. Add CHECK `INTERVAL_DELTA` ⇒ kWh, positive interval, nonnegative canonical value (or UNAVAILABLE/null); connector ID must match the bound asset in S2 acceptance, and partial index `(site_id,session_id,connector_id,source_time,id)` for EV measurements. This revision anchors code vocabulary and fixture check (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/models.py:26-50,59-172,255-264`; `backend/migrations/versions/0001_foundation_identity.py:66`). | Refuse while EV measurement rows exist; restore 0020 CHECK/index, retain historical Foundation readers. |
+| `0025_w2_ev_session` / W2-06 | `ev_session` (`id`, tenant/site, `charger_asset_id`,`connector_asset_id`, `external_session_id text` length 1..128, `first_start_evidence_id uuid`, `anchor_time`, `created_at`; scoped FKs; UNIQUE `(site_id,charger_asset_id,connector_asset_id,external_session_id)`). `ev_session_revision` (`id`, tenant/site/session, `revision_no int>0`, `assessment_revision_id`, `analytic_run_id`, `basis_sha256`, `state` `COMPLETED|OBSERVED_ABORT|INCOMPLETE|CONTRADICTORY`, `start_at`, `end_at NULL`, `start_bound_low/high`, `end_bound_low/high NULL`, `supported_kwh numeric NULL`, `unknown_meter_seconds int>=0`, `fault_evidence_id NULL`, `reason_codes jsonb array`, `created_at`; CHECK ordered bounds, end>=start, nonnegative kWh, completed/abort requires explicit terminal event; UNIQUE `(session_id,revision_no)` and `(session_id,basis_sha256)`). `ev_session_evidence` (`revision_id`, tenant/site, `evidence_id`, `use` `SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED`, `reason NULL`, PK `(revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, scoped FKs). `ev_meter_interval` (`id`, tenant/site/session revision, connector asset, `start_at`,`end_at`, `energy_kwh numeric>=0`, `error_kwh numeric>=0`, `evidence_id`, `use` `SUPPORT|EXCLUDED`, `reason NULL`; CHECK start<end and SUPPORT requires zero overlap with other SUPPORT intervals of same session revision via GiST exclusion; UNIQUE `(revision_id,evidence_id)`; scoped FKs). Index `(site_id,anchor_time,id)`, `(site_id,connector_asset_id,anchor_time)`, `(site_id,session_id,revision_no DESC)` and `(site_id,start_at,end_at)`; append-only triggers. Extend `finding` as 0022 does for cold: `subject_asset_id` scoped FK, nullable generator asset, domain CHECK permitting EV with subject asset; retain DISPATCH requirements. If 0022 already added the generic columns, only widen its domain CHECK. Index `(tenant_id,site_id,domain,subject_asset_id,anchor_start)`. `recompute_job` domain permits EV; enqueue for accepted EV roles/events and late facts. | Refuse while EV rows/Findings or EV jobs exist; drop EV tables/indexes, restore domain CHECK only if no EV Finding. |
+| `0026_w2_ev_money` / W2-07 | Widen `financial_assumption_revision.assumption_key` CHECK to include `ev_gross_tariff_per_kwh`; its unit CHECK permits `currency/kWh` exactly for that key, diesel remains `currency/L`; positive finite tariff, ISO currency, existing succession, correction, author, valid/recorded and overlap rules remain. No default revision (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:38-102`). Add `ev_consequence_revision` (`id`, tenant/site, `finding_revision_id NULL`, `assessment_revision_id`, `revision_no int>0`, `basis_sha256`, `technical_input_sha256`, `formula_version='ev-gross-tariff/1'`, `window_start/end`, `quantity_kwh numeric NULL`, `tariff_revision_id NULL`, `tariff_value numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`, `amount numeric NULL`, `state` `ESTIMATED|UNPRICED|WITHHELD`, `reason`, `source_gate`, `overlap_group`, `recorded_at`; CHECK ordered window, nonnegative finite quantity/tariff, ESTIMATED iff quantity/tariff/currency/amount present and `amount=quantity_kwh*tariff_value`, otherwise amount/currency null; scoped FKs to assumption and assessment; unique `(site_id,window_start,window_end,basis_sha256)` and `(site_id,window_start,window_end,revision_no)`). Add `ev_consequence_session` (`consequence_revision_id`, tenant/site, `session_revision_id`, `quantity_kwh`, PK pair, scoped FKs) and `ev_consequence_evidence` (`consequence_revision_id`, tenant/site, `evidence_id`, PK pair, scoped FKs). Index `(site_id,recorded_at DESC)` and `(site_id,window_start,window_end,revision_no DESC)`; append-only triggers. The old fuel consequence CHECK remains fuel-only (`backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-118`). | Refuse with EV tariff revisions or EV consequences; drop new tables, restore original assumption CHECKs. No cascade. |
+
+`ev_session_revision` holds a selected assessment snapshot, never a mutable session status. Existing `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision` retain exact basis/source lineage; EV `analytic_id=ev-session/1`. A Finding is created only for an observed fault-aborted session; `finding_key=sha256(tenant,site,'EV_INTERRUPTION',charger,connector,session_id,first_start_evidence_id)` and `subject_asset_id=connector`. Later terminal/late records append revisions; invalidation uses `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`, never deletion. One Finding per scoped session, no Finding for missing end (`backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:38-125`; `backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-107`).
+
+## 2. W2-05a Foundation, role set, readiness
+
+Register immutable `ev-roles-1` in the site-type role registry; bind by effective Foundation and source mapping revisions (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). Topology is Site feed → Site import meter → EV charger → EV connector; each connector has exactly one parent charger, each charger one feed, each asset ID distinct, no topology cycles or cross-Site links. Charger/connector serial labels are display metadata, never session IDs. Site import limit and charger/connector ratings are effective Foundation facts, not measured capacity. Connector enable schedule is a time-valid authored schedule in Foundation (`weekly local intervals`, Site timezone, explicit UTC offset resolution for DST); W2 uses it for configured service hours only, not an availability percentage. Reject overlapping intervals, nonexistent/ambiguous local instants and enabled connector without a declared charger/feed/meter. No stationary battery unless explicitly declared.
+
+| Role / asset | v4 kind, unit, cardinality and gate |
+| --- | --- |
+| `ev_session_event` / EV_CONNECTOR | `EV_SESSION_START|END|ABORT`, one event channel per connector; session ID + connector ID required, `correlation_id=session_id`; required for outcome. |
+| `ev_connector_fault` / EV_CONNECTOR | `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULT|CLEAR` event, one per connector; optional for completion, required for fault-caused interruption. Fault event's optional session ID must match same connector when attached. |
+| `ev_connector_state` / EV_CONNECTOR | point `AVAILABLE|PLUGGED|AUTHORIZING|CHARGING|FAULTED|DISABLED`, `state`, one per connector, declared max 1-minute cadence; required for observed service-state context, never substitutes for terminal event. |
+| `ev_connector_energy` / EV_CONNECTOR | nonnegative `INTERVAL_DELTA`, kWh, one meter channel per connector, 5-minute half-open slots; required for supported energy. W2-00 v4 MEASUREMENT currently allows `STATE_AT_INSTANT|INTERVAL_MEAN`; extend its **v4 measurement-kind allowlist** with `INTERVAL_DELTA`, numeric `kWh`, positive interval duration, nonnegative value, explicit availability/quality; v3 bytes/semantics unchanged. Mapping only accepts this kind on this role. |
+| `ev_connector_power` / EV_CONNECTOR | nonnegative `INTERVAL_MEAN`, kW, one per connector, optional corroboration; never replaces energy meter. |
+| `ev_site_import` / METER | nonnegative `INTERVAL_MEAN`, kW, ONE per Site feed, 5-minute aligned slots; required only for a bounded observed site-power comparison, not for session outcome/energy. |
+| `ev_requested_power`,`ev_allocated_limit` / EV_CONNECTOR | nonnegative `INTERVAL_MEAN`, kW, optional independently reported controller channels; both plus synchronized site import/limit needed before a power-constraint explanation. Private vehicle request is never accepted demand evidence. |
+
+Each role has registered source/device/signal, same-Site asset, exact unit and mapping, effective validity, cadence and clock bound. One physical signal cannot silently serve incompatible roles. `INTERVAL_DELTA` is an interval energy fact, never a cumulative counter. If a vendor supplies cumulative meter values, the labelled peer adapter converts only adjacent monotone readings with exact timestamps, meter reset/rollover declaration and error bound; reset or ambiguous jump yields no delta. Missing role, wrong unit/asset, overlapping bindings, ambiguous charger parent, invalid schedule, or cadence > configured ceiling refuse publication with typed configuration error; old Foundation revisions remain selectable. Readiness messages are fixed: `CONNECTOR_NOT_DECLARED`, `FEED_OR_LIMIT_MISSING`, `METER_NOT_BOUND`, `SESSION_EVENTS_NOT_BOUND`, `STATUS_NOT_BOUND`, `SITE_IMPORT_NOT_BOUND`, `SOURCE_NOT_CONTACTED`, `COMMISSIONING_UNCONFIRMED`. First six are configuration/capability, contact is S7 receipt state, commissioning needs an actual record. `READY_TO_REPORT` means configured, never commissioned/healthy (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`; `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:38-57`).
+
+## 3. W2-05b versioned charger/session producer
+
+Add `ev-charger/1` `ModelSpec`, `ev-publication/1`, `ev-observation/1` to the part-1 closed host registry, preserving all existing model identities (`host/execution_adapter.py:123-149`; `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:59-69`). Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. Frozen run identity includes Foundation/binding/source IDs, scenario SHA, pack/profile/observation versions, UTC window and seed. Stage seals only public v4 reports; registered Commit → SQLite outbox → authenticated Batch V2 → S2 accepted evidence. No direct evidence/Finding seed, Lab private state, vehicle/customer identity or hidden demand in envelopes (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).
+
+**Discrete model.** One-minute UTC step, exact decimal arithmetic; default fixture has two 60 kW connectors, 80 kW site import cap, both enabled 08:00–16:00 UTC on 2026-10-06, site non-EV base load 0 kW. State per connector: `UNPLUGGED|PLUGGED|AUTHORIZING|CHARGING|FAULTED`, session ID or null, authorization result, requested kW, allocated kW, meter accumulator kWh, fault latch. Private forcing: plug/unplug, authorization accept/deny, request schedule, fault/clear, grid interruption, publication dropout/latency. Initial unplugged, no session/fault, accumulator 0. At each minute: apply forcing ordered `(time,connector,kind)`; a plug assigns next deterministic scoped session ID; authorization takes 1 minute; acceptance emits START when CHARGING begins; denial produces no START and is a contextual observed state, not a failed session. Allocate shared available power `max(0,site_limit-base_load)` by connector ID ascending in round-robin 1 kW quanta among charging connectors up to `min(request,connector_limit,charger_limit)`; persist round-robin pointer so no connector monopolizes successive minutes. `E_next=E+allocated_kW/60`, exact kWh. A fault latches FAULTED, emits FAULT and ABORT for an active started session; explicit user unplug emits END (completed) only after active charge and no fault; grid interruption pauses allocation and connector state, it does not invent ABORT. Fault clear requires explicit forcing and emits CLEAR; a new plug creates a new session. Simultaneous END/ABORT or START twice for one ID is prohibited by simulator sanity guard. Fail run on negative energy, allocated sum above available/site/connector limits, energy residual, state with no valid session, duplicate conflicting forcing or non-monotone time.
+
+**Public transform.** `ev-publication/1`: connector state at transitions and every minute; connector interval energy delta over aligned five-minute `[start,end)` slots (including zero while an active accepted session pauses), with session ID carried in the role-bound measurement extension; power mean per slot optional; site import mean per slot; session/fault events at observed transitions. To make meter correlation explicit, v4 measurement payload gains optional paired `session_id,connector_id` keys: both required for `ev_connector_energy`, connector must match role asset; existing canonical v4 cold payload without those keys remains readable, while v3 remains byte-for-byte unchanged. The parser emits nulls internally when keys are absent. `source_time=interval_end` for interval reports. At a session boundary inside a slot, split at the boundary into nonoverlapping subintervals so no kWh crosses session identities; null-session energy cannot be assigned to a session. Round accumulated energy to 0.001 kWh, half-even, by differencing rounded end/start accumulator, making published slot deltas additive; meter bound 0.001 kWh per boundary. Event IDs derive from scenario SHA, channel and transition ordinal; retransmission preserves IDs/bytes. Observation gap drops a channel report, never changes the world; delayed report retains source time and message identity. Mapping `simulator-lab-gateway-ev/1` is an exact allowlist for `(charger-controller,session-event)`, `(connector-controller,fault-event)`, `(connector-controller,state)`, `(connector-meter,energy)`, `(connector-meter,power)`, `(site-meter,import)`, `(connector-controller,requested-power)`, `(connector-controller,allocated-limit)` with above kinds/units/cadences; no wildcard (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).
+
+| Scenario `config/scenarios/ev-*.yaml` / seed 20517 | Forcing and expected *public* distinction |
+| --- | --- |
+| `complete` | Plug, authorize, charge, unplug; START + END and exact nonoverlapping meter slots → COMPLETED, bounded kWh. |
+| `interrupted` | Fault during charge → FAULT + ABORT with same connector/session; pre-fault meter slots remain delivered, no inferred unmet demand. |
+| `fault` | Fault before authorization on one connector, then CLEAR; no START/ABORT/Finding for the rejected attempt; other connector can complete. |
+| `missing-meter` | One energy report dropped while events remain; completed outcome but partial energy with explicit unknown interval; no filled kWh. |
+| `late-record` | Hold terminal END or one meter report until after first assessment; same source time, new accepted fact triggers recompute and new revision. |
+| `contradictory` | Distinct accepted START/END claims for same scoped ID with incompatible times, or overlapping good meter intervals; capability CONTRADICTORY, no resolved amount. Same event ID with changed bytes is S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`, never a second accepted fact. |
+
+All scenarios use `ev-scenario/1`, explicit ordered forcing, frozen window/seed and profile. No target 12/10/1/1 or 120 kWh/$36 is injected; display only replay-derived numbers (`frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js:433-450`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:1-32`).
+
+## 4. W2-06 session correlation, meter sum, Finding, currentness
+
+Select only accepted, GOOD, clock-attested records with valid effective Foundation/binding and exact `(tenant,site,charger,connector,session_id)`; EVENT session ID comes from W2-00, energy identity from the v4 measurement extension above. Require one START, at most one terminal END or ABORT, ordered by source time with <=30-second declared cross-source clock error; a terminal without START remains orphan context, not a session. An END is COMPLETED only when same scoped ID, no competing ABORT, and no contradictory state; explicit ABORT proves OBSERVED_ABORT, while contemporaneous fault on the same connector/session is additionally required for a **fault-associated** Finding. Fault alone does not prove an abort. Missing terminal is INCOMPLETE regardless of apparently idle status; conflicting good terminal/start times or incompatible connector identity is CONTRADICTORY. `SUSPECT` and unbound reports are context/excluded with reasons, never settle outcomes. Repeat events are deduped by W2-00 and cross-tenant/session collisions cannot join.
+
+For each session, intersect GOOD connector `INTERVAL_DELTA` reports with `[start,terminal)` and same session ID; accept only wholly contained, nonoverlapping half-open intervals with finite `0<=kWh<=connector_max_kW*duration_hours+2*meter_error_bound`, and matching exact source. Do **not** pro-rate a report spanning a boundary, choose between conflicting duplicates, substitute power integration or infer zero for an absent slot. `supported_kwh=sum(exact accepted deltas)`; `unknown_meter_seconds` is the union duration of uncovered active 5-minute slots, clipped to session bounds. A delivered-energy total is **complete** only if every active slot is covered (partial boundary slots must have a split report) and no contradiction; otherwise report known partial sum with `energy_complete=false` and unknown time, never call it total delivered. If no supported intervals, quantity null rather than zero. Session outcome counts use START denominator, separately completed/observed abort/incomplete/contradictory; orphan records are separate. No availability percentage or site constraint cause in W2. Synchronized site import below limit is context only; a power-constraint claim requires independent accepted requested and allocated-limit signals plus site import over the same slots, and W2 still labels competing causes unknown (`.agent/architect-W2-design.md:37-49`).
+
+`ev-session/1` assessment basis SHA sorts accepted evidence IDs+content digests, excluded/context IDs+reasons, effective Foundation/binding/mapping IDs, model/rule versions, `[from,to)`, `known_at` and source classification. Session revision and its evidence/interval links append atomically with `assessment_revision` and any FindingRevision. A fault-abort Finding uses the stable key above, with exact fault/abort/START evidence and limitation “fault observed; component cause and unmet demand unknown.” A completed late END supersedes prior INCOMPLETE by new revision; a contradictory terminal withdraws previous current conclusion. Worker enqueue on **every** newly accepted relevant EV report, including late source time; until latest applicable job settles, previous projection reads `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`, with historical exact revision separately selectable. For current status, latest needed connector state <=2 minutes old at read time and terminal/event sources within declared cadence; old closed sessions may remain historical conclusions. Current service-window coverage uses one-minute status slots and explicit `known_seconds/unknown_seconds`; no available-time inference over gaps >2 minutes. Source gate follows S4-05 exact revision provenance; generated and authored evidence are labelled and mixed/unknown blocks a current positive (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15,4165-4188`).
+
+## 5. W2-07 tariff scenario and claim gate
+
+`ev_gross_tariff_per_kwh` is a Site time-valid, recorded revision in `currency/kWh`, with authored basis/source; no default and no billing/collection identity. A window may price only supported energy in completed or observed-abort sessions, each with complete meter coverage and settled current assessment; no session interval is counted twice. For every included meter interval, select exactly one tariff revision whose **valid** range covers it and whose **recorded** range is visible at `known_at`; W2 v1 requires the same revision and currency across the entire priced window, otherwise `UNPRICED/PRICE_GAP_OR_CHANGE` (no averaging). Formula `amount = Σ exact interval kWh × tariff value`, unrounded in storage; display rounding only. `quantity_kwh` and exact session revision/accepted meter evidence/tariff revision IDs are pinned. No lost-sales estimate: private requested power, incomplete sessions and fault downtime do not measure counterfactual demand. An aborted session can contribute **delivered** pre-abort energy, never lost energy. W1 LR current-money gate and S4-05 coherent source gate apply: pending/late/revised assessment, conflicting meter, historical cutoff, mixed/unknown provenance, unsupported tariff, or missing price → `WITHHELD` or `UNPRICED`, null amount (not zero). Historical amount may appear labelled historical only under its exact old basis, never as today's current money. `overlap_group=site/window/tariff` prevents adding the same meter intervals through multiple Findings; Site scenario is one window revision, not per-Finding additive totals (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:38-102`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).
+
+## 6. Selected reads and refusals
+
+All routes are tenant-authenticated Site-code reads. Except Foundation/replay, require UTC half-open `from,to`, max 31 days, optional UTC `known_at` (default latest committed watermark); a historical cutoff is labelled historical. Common response `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. Watermark pins latest included S2 decision time + assessment revision; lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, default limit 50/max 200, `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared error `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/Lab gate/source-gated detail, 404 missing/wrong Site type or record, 409 unresolved selected revision or conflicting current recompute, 422 bad parameter/window/selector, 503 ledger/projection unavailable. Valid evidence gaps return 200 with UNKNOWN/null and reasons. No endpoint returns private forcing/oracle, customer/vehicle or a fabricated zero (`.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:83-107`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:54-59,157-161`).
+
+| Route / slice | Additional fields and selection |
+| --- | --- |
+| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/foundation?at=` / 05a | Effective `foundation_revision_id,valid_from,valid_to,feed:{asset_id,limit_kw},chargers[{asset_id,max_kw,connectors:[{asset_id,max_kw,enabled_schedule}]}],topology:{nodes,edges},bindings[{role,asset_id,source_id,unit,cadence,valid_from,valid_to}],readiness:{state,messages},commissioning:{state,evidence_ids},contact:{state,as_of}`; `at` defaults now. |
+| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/replay?publication_id=` / 05b | Owner-gated Lab public `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; exact registered publication only. |
+| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/sessions` and `.../sessions/{id}` / 06 | Paged `session_id,charger_asset_id,connector_asset_id,revision_id,start_bounds,end_bounds,state,supported_kwh,energy_complete,unknown_meter_seconds,reason_codes,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links`; detail adds ordered `meter_intervals[{start,end,kwh,evidence_id,use,reason}]`, events, exclusions and revision history. External session ID only in labelled exact detail. |
+| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/findings` and `.../findings/{id}` / 06 | Paged `finding_id,revision_id,session_id,subject_asset_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links`; detail includes immutable revision history and exact source basis. |

+| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/evidence` and `.../evidence/analysis` / 07 | Summary `capabilities[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage}],sources[{id,role,contact,fact_age}],links`; Analysis paged `accepted_records[{evidence_id,family,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,session_id,exclusion}],claim_rules,source_provenance`. W2-00 `/evidence/records` remains exact source link. |
+| `GET /api/sites/{code}/ev/financials` and `.../financial-assumptions` / 07 | `consequences[{revision_id,state,quantity_kwh,tariff_revision_id,tariff_value,currency,amount,reason,source_gate,overlap_group,exact_basis}],not_included:[lost_sales,collections,margin,repair]`; assumptions `items[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,basis,source}],missing_keys`. Existing assumption publication route accepts new key with same authorization/idempotency rules. |
